## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 실제 작업을 맡길 수 있는 개인 AI 파트너예요. 예를 들어 "이 저장소에서 깨진 테스트 고쳐 줘"라고 하시면 제가 작업을 열어서 끝나면 알려 드릴게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 네, 고마워요. 내일 아침에 봐요.
  [also used tools] routine_list
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPとUDPの一番の違いは、**TCPは届いたかを確認し、順番どおり確実に届ける**のに対し、**UDPは確認や再送をせず、速さを優先する**ことです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales antes de compartirlos, por ejemplo para ponerte al día con la rama principal o limpiar un PR que aún no has publicado. Usa merge cuando la rama ya está compartida o publicada, porque no reescribe el historial y no rompe el trabajo de otras personas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 面向连接、保证可靠有序地送达；UDP 无连接、只管尽快发出去，不保证送达和顺序。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to bring it up to date and keep history linear before you merge. Merge when the branch is shared, or when you want the record of how and when the work came together. Never rebase commits others have already pulled.
```

### group-split-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: ~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。
  [partner ack line] 好，登录页分三件做：先换输入框组件，再接短信验证码，最后补端到端测试，一件做完再开下一件，每件完成了告诉你。
  [opened a task] title: 登录页：换新输入框组件 | group: 登录页重做 | place: {"kind":"repo","device":"local","repoPath":"~/dev/ducter"} | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: 用户原话："~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。" ⏎  ⏎ 这是第 1 件（共 3 件）。只做第 1 件：把登录表单换成新的输入框组件。 ⏎ 新输入框组件以仓库里现有的为准；如果找不到明确的组件，停下来用 needs_input 问用户，不要自己发明一个。 ⏎  ⏎ 完成标准（都要满足）： ⏎ - 登录表单里所有输入框都改用新的输入框组件，页面渲染正常； ⏎ - 跑通相关的测试或构建，把命令和结果作为证据写进报告。 ⏎  ⏎ 不要做（范围外）： ⏎ - 不接短信验证码（那是第 2 件）； ⏎ - 不改登录页以外的页面； ⏎ - 不合并到主分支、不推送、不开 PR。在自己的分支上提交即可。 ⏎  ⏎ 地点：~/dev/ducter
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：接短信验证码 | group: 登录页重做
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：补端到端测试 | group: 登录页重做
--- under the user's message: 1. 登录页：换新输入框组件 (working); 2. 登录页：接短信验证码 (working); 3. 登录页：补端到端测试 (working)
```

### group-words-zh

```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] `bun test` 后面直接跟文件路径就行：

```sh
bun test test/lru.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段字符的测试文件：

```sh
bun test lru
```

如果还想只跑某一个用例，加 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）：

```sh
bun test test/lru.test.ts -t "过期"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change to a database gets written down before the change is applied to the main data. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart to recover anything that was committed but not yet saved to the data files.

### past-rust-haswell-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 我上个月在 haswell 上那个 Rust 构建的，再接着把增量构建也弄快
  [partner ack line] 好，接着上个月那个「haswell 上 Rust 构建太慢」往下做增量构建，弄清楚了告诉你。
  [sent to task rust_haswell] 用户说："我上个月在 haswell 上那个 Rust 构建的，再接着把增量构建也弄快"。上次已经把冷构建从 14 分钟降到 6 分钟（sccache + mold），增量构建还没动。

要做的：
- 在 haswell 上测一下当前的增量构建耗时（改一个文件后重新构建），记录基线。
- 找出增量构建慢的原因，并做能让增量构建变快的改动（比如 incremental 编译设置、sccache 配置、链接器、依赖拆分等，以实测为准）。
- 改完再测一次，给出前后对比的数字。

完成标准：增量构建有明显提速，并附上前后耗时的实测结果。现有的冷构建不能变慢。

不在范围内：不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR。改动放在现有分支上提交即可。
  [also used tools] task_find
```

  [partner message] Done. The board is set up and filled with the last two nights (181k and 182k writes/s, both with 0 compaction stalls). New runs will add to it.
  [an app card under the message: made "Nightly hawdb write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2043 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">Waiting for the first run</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">Writes per second and p99 latency, every night at 23:00</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="nights"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty">Each night's result will list here.</div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  function render(nights) {
    var list = document.getElementById('nights');
    var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    if (!nights || !nights.length) {
      empty.style.display = '';
      document.getElementById('latest').textContent = 'Waiting for the first run';
      document.getElementById('latest-sub').textContent = 'Writes per second and p99 latency, every night at 23:00';
      return;
    }
    empty.style.display = 'none';
    nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function (n) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main"></div><div class="k-row-value"></div>';
      row.querySelector('.k-row-main').textContent = n.date;
      row.querySelector('.k-row-sub') || null;
      var sub = document.createElement('div');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + n.stalls;
      row.querySelector('.k-row-main').appendChild(sub);
      row.querySelector('.k-row-value').textContent = n.writes;
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
    document.getElementById('latest').textContent = last.writes;
    document.getElementById('latest-sub').textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls + ' · ' + last.date;
  }
  render(ducter.get('nights'));
  ducter.onChange(function () { render(ducter.get('nights')); });
  ducter.summary('Last night: ' + ((ducter.get('nights') || []).slice(-1)[0] || {}).writes);
})();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"date":"Night 2","writes":"181k writes/s","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0},{"date":"Night 3","writes":"182k writes/s","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

```

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没记</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>
</main>
<style>
.k-sum-value{font-size:44px;font-weight:700;color:var(--d-ink)}
.k-sum-label{color:var(--d-muted)}
.k-sum-sub{color:var(--d-soft)}
.k-app{padding:16px;background:var(--d-bg);color:var(--d-ink);min-height:100vh;box-sizing:border-box}
.k-h{margin:20px 0 8px;font-size:16px;color:var(--d-muted)}
</style>
<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+n.toLocaleString("en-US")}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+(Number(x.yuan)||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=fmt(total);
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length? xs.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  ducter.summary(xs.length? xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total) : "还没记");
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  if(!xs.length){const e=document.createElement("div");e.className="k-empty";e.textContent="还没有花销";list.appendChild(e);return;}
  for(const x of xs){
    const r=document.createElement("div");r.className="k-row";
    const m=document.createElement("div");m.className="k-row-main";m.textContent=x.what;
    const s=document.createElement("div");s.className="k-row-sub";s.textContent=x.day;
    const v=document.createElement("div");v.className="k-row-value";v.textContent=fmt(Number(x.yuan)||0);
    r.append(m,s,v);list.appendChild(r);
  }
}
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

  .k-row { cursor: pointer; }
  .k-row-remove { margin-top: 8px; width: 100%; }
  .k-row-open .k-row-remove { display: block; }
  .k-row-remove { display: none; }

  .k-card .k-btn { margin-top: 14px; …    list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }

  function buildCats() {
    const wrap = $('cats');
    CATS.forEach((c) => {
      const b = document.createElement('button');
      b.className = 'k-chip';
      b.textContent = c;
      b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(c === cat));
      b.onclick = () => {
        cat = c;
        [...wrap.children].forEach((x) => x.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(x.textContent === c)));
      };
      wrap.appendChild(b);
    });
  }

  function save() {
    ducter.set('spends', data);
    render();
  }

  $('add').onclick = () => {
    const v = parseFloat($('amt').value.replace(',', '.'));
    if (!(v > 0)) { $('amt').focus(); return; }
    data.push({
      id: Date.now(),
      amount: Math.round(v * 100) / 100,
      cat: cat,
      note: $('note').value.trim(),
      date: today()
    });
    $('amt').value = '';
    $('note').value = '';
    save();
  };

  buildCats();
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('spends')).then((v) => {
    data = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  });
  ducter.onChange(() => {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('spends')).then((v) => {
      data = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
      render();
    });
  });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var sel = -1;
  function today() {
    var d = new Date();
    return d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  }
  function nowHM() {
    var d = new Date();
    return Str … mentById('empty').hidden = items.length > 0;
    document.getElementById('total').innerHTML = items.length
      ? '<span>' + total + ' ml</span>'
      : '<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = items.length
      ? items.length + ' 次,平均 ' + Math.round(total / items.length) + ' ml'
      : '几次,每次多少';
    ducter.summary(items.length ? items.length + ' 笔,合计 ' + total + ' ml' : '今天还没记');
  }
  document.getElementById('t').value = nowHM();
  document.getElementById('add').onclick = function () {
    var t = document.getElementById('t').value.trim();
    var ml = parseFloat(document.getElementById('ml').value);
    if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(t) || !(ml > 0)) return;
    var all = feeds();
    all.push({ d: today(), t: t, ml: ml });
    ducter.set('feeds', all);
    document.getElementById('ml').value = '';
    document.getElementById('t').value = nowHM();
  };
  document.getElementById('del').onclick = function () {
    if (sel < 0) return;
    var all = feeds();
    all.splice(sel, 1);
    ducter.set('feeds', all);
    sel = -1;
    document.getElementById('del-card').hidden = true;
  };
  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"d":"2026-11-21","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"d":"2026-11-21","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}

  .k-row label { display: flex; gap: 12px; align-items: flex-start; width: 100%; cursor: pointer; }
  .k-row input[type=checkbox] { width: 20px; height: 20px; margin-top: 2px; accent-color: var(--d-accent); flex: none; }
  .k-row-main { color: var(--d-ink); }
  .k-row-sub { color: var(--d-muted); font-size: 13px; margin-top: 2px; }
  .k-row-value { color: var(--d-soft); font-size: 13px; white-space: nowrap; margin-left: auto; padding-left: 8px; }
  .k-row.done { opacity: .5; }
  .k-row.done .k-row-main { text-decoration: line-through; }
</style>
<script>
  var items = [];
  function load() {
    items = ducter.get('prs') || [];
  }
  function render() {
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    var open = 0;
    items.forEach(function (it, i) {
      if (!it.done) open++;
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-r … = 'checkbox';
      box.checked = !!it.done;
      box.addEventListener('change', function () {
        items[i].done = box.checked;
        ducter.set('prs', items);
        render();
      });
      var text = document.createElement('div');
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = it.pr + ' · ' + it.title;
      var sub = document.createElement('div');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = it.repo;
      text.appendChild(main);
      text.appendChild(sub);
      var value = document.createElement('div');
      value.className = 'k-row-value';
      value.textContent = it.done ? 'Landed' : it.status;
      label.appendChild(box);
      label.appendChild(text);
      label.appendChild(value);
      row.appendChild(label);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    var landed = items.length - open;
    document.getElementById('n').textContent = open;
    document.getElementById('s').textContent = 'of ' + items.length + ' PRs · ' + landed + ' landed';
    ducter.summary(open + ' open, ' + landed + ' landed');
  }
  ducter.onChange(function () { load(); render(); });
  load();
  render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"pr":"wey-gu/ducter#412","repo":"wey-gu/ducter","title":"Compaction cut","status":"Waiting on Weizhen's review","done":false},{"pr":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","repo":"nowledge-co/mem","title":"Retry on 502","status":"CI failing","done":false},{"pr":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb","title":"Flaky compaction test","status":"Waiting on Alice","done":false},{"pr":"wey-gu/ducter#418","repo":"wey-gu/ducter","title":"Apps bar","status":"Draft","done":false}]}
